FOUR ONCE-ONLY SEARCH ARMS · 18 OCTOBER 2026

Do complete lemmas help the search?

No point proof; conventional proof outcomes below.

The point pair and conventional empty control retained exactly the same four source-ground records, proposals, structural checks and full inventory preimages. Both point arms stopped without a proof; neither started a lemma-provider call. The conventional empty control produced a checked 20-line proof. The conventional inventory result exceeded the declared 4 MiB completed-packet cap and was not retained; its proof outcome is unavailable, so the conventional pair cannot be compared.

The receiver asks for \(\forall n\;\operatorname{add}\left(\mathit{n},S\left(S\left(0\right)\right)\right)=S\left(S\left(\mathit{n}\right)\right)\) from the original two addition axioms and empty assumptions. This is a bounded arithmetic search experiment, without induction. Each arm uses the same original mathematical language, whole donor packet, executable capsule and native resource limits. The no-inventory arms pay the same acquisition and structural checking costs, then disable the inventory offers.

The peak memory limit is \(512\;\mathrm{MiB}\). The gray bar is failed-call telemetry; it carries no admitted proof result and cannot serve as a conventional inventory comparison. These are one-shot observations, with no rerun or statistical speed claim.

The arms are configured as inventory on/off comparisons within each search strategy. The conventional strategy searches a specialized logical rewrite fragment; point search maintains the frontier graph and branches by the required generation order. Their search spaces and matching quotas differ. The point pair did not reach the family-provider callback at all: both have zero started provider calls. The source review shows that the pre-provider census and validation path is shared by both modes. The treatment-only branch path was not reached. The different event counts in these one-shot wall-clock runs do not establish a treatment effect or its cause, and these traces do not test successful lemma matching. The conventional proof is useful evidence of automatic proof discovery, but does not establish a GCTS contribution. No trained RL policy, learned marking, general theorem-proving advantage or complete-board result is claimed.

The shared complete-root inventory

The source proof was discovered by an earlier conventional search. Complete ancestry is retained for each returned root. These records are conditional proof recipes, with their original variable scope and assumption context. They become neither new axioms nor globally free facts. Every actual use must compile into checked primitive rules. The remaining scheduled source roots retain UNKNOWN dispositions.

What the parallel agents contributed

The agent conventional_library authored both the provider/engine changes and the conventional control. The agent conventional_control_review authored the point-arm driver and later audited the saved results. The agent receiver_driver_review reviewed the exact sources and traced the pre-provider path. Root authored the common runner and report, issued separate once-only grants, ran the actual searches and admitted only closed evidence. The review packets disclose earlier source authorship; a report or saved-data review does not requalify an author’s own source. The reviewer caught and rejected a result-certificate variable collision before execution. A later integration pilot exited with a call-interface error before point construction or search: Root had passed a class to a function-only guarded interface. That failed, consumed pilot remains in the archive as a fifth attempt outside the four matched arms. A narrow constructor wrapper repair received a separate source review; the four matched arms use that new frozen capsule. No search rule or resource limit changed. The preserved sources, failure receipt and exact patches are included.

The next work is to understand the shared pre-provider census and validation cost while preserving complete frontier bookkeeping and the required scheduler, and to repair the lossless result protocol so it can carry every allowed conventional result within the original whole-result allowance. These require new frozen sources and review; the consumed runs will not be repeated. Successful, useful cluster selection remains unobserved. Faster source checking or a finite inventory pass alone is insufficient evidence of better proof search.

The comparison record was frozen before the independent audit; its pending-review field is historical. The linked saved-data audit and summary record the completed review. The archive preserves original paths as metadata. Reproduction requires a fresh explicit plan and compatible declared runtime; the consumed actual directories must not be rerun. No scientific module was imported or evaluated while rendering this report.

GCTS research notebook. Finite evidence is kept separate from universal validity, complete point-board tiling, learned heuristics and performance claims.
